<h3 id="223">[223] Scalable On-Hardware Training of Quantum Neural Networks and Application to Clinical Data Imputation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03517](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03517) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Natansh Mathur, Panagiotis Kl. Barkoutsos, Masako Yamada, Martin Roetteler, Iordanis Kerenidis
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Quantum Machine Learning, Data Imputation, Medical AI
- **Summary**: 针对量子神经网络硬件训练梯度成本高的问题，提出结合蝴蝶电路、逐层训练和并行参数移位的框架，将电路评估数从O(n²)降至O(log n)。在IonQ硬件上训练16量子比特模型，在MIMIC-III数据集上达到与经典基线相当的性能。

<h3 id="224">[224] Post-Hoc Robustness for Model-Based Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03521](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03521) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Siemen Herremans, Ali Anwar, Siegfried Mercelis
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Adversarial Robustness, Model-Based RL
- **Summary**: 针对强化学习在环境扰动下的鲁棒性问题，提出推理时事后鲁棒化方法。利用学习模型和名义策略，通过模型预测控制结合对抗rollout进行策略改进。在扰动MuJoCo环境中显著提升鲁棒性。

<h3 id="225">[225] High-Precision APT Malware Attribution with Out-of-Scope Resilience</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03523](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03523) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Peter Williams, Adam Sobey, Erisa Karafili
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Cybersecurity, Malware Detection, Out-of-Distribution Detection
- **Summary**: 现有APT恶意软件归因方法通常作为封闭集分类器，无法处理训练中未出现的新APT组。本文提出基于排名二元分类器和显式弃权的归因方法，对每个APT组训练两个分类器并按验证性能排序应用，仅在证据充分时归因。在APT恶意软件数据集上实现了更高精度，在87%测试样本来自未参与训练组的挑战设置中，对94%的域外样本弃权，同时分类样本的精度达92%。

<h3 id="226">[226] When Should the Teacher Move? Temporal Coupling and Stability in Self On-Policy Distillation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03532](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03532) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haowei Guo, Baolong Bi, Ruicheng Zhang, Bingqian Sun, Wentao Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Self-Distillation, Training Stability
- **Summary**: 在自在线蒸馏中，教师更新调度（时间耦合）作为稳定性变量未被系统研究。通过控制实验发现冻结周期是关键结构属性，并提出巩固门控教师刷新（CGTR），在奖励改进和长度安全联合证据下门控刷新。CGTR实现零崩溃，在化学、生物、物理和工具使用四个任务上均取得最佳分数。

<h3 id="227">[227] \textsc{CR-Seg}: Attention-Guided and CoT-Enhanced Coarse-to-Refined Reasoning Segmentation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03564](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03564) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yifan Cao, Xiaocui Yang, Faxian Wan, Shi Feng, Daling Wang, Yifei Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reasoning Segmentation, Chain-of-Thought, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 现有推理分割方法依赖语义令牌或空间提示，存在跨模态对齐困难或语义丢失问题。本文提出CR-Seg两阶段框架，设计EAP模块提取注意力图用于粗定位，并引入全局到局部思维链（GLCoT）逐步推理。实验验证了方法的有效性。

<h3 id="228">[228] Efficient Transformer-Based Localized Patch Sampling for Choroid Plexus Segmentation in Multiple Sclerosis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03566](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03566) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Po-Jui Lu, Alessandro Cagol, Mario Ocampo-Pineda, Federico Spagnolo, Marina Mastantuono, Andreea-Alexandra Aldea, Jannis Müller, Özgür Yaldizli, Matthias Weigel, Lester Melie-Garcia, Roberta Magliozzi, Maria Pia Sormani, Ludwig Kappos, Jens Kuhle, Cristina Granziera
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Medical Imaging, Image Segmentation
- **Summary**: 手动分割侧脑室脉络丛（LVCP）耗时且限制临床应用。本文提出基于SwinUNETR和局部小块采样的自动分割管线，在MPRAGE和FLAIR联合输入下DSC达0.868，计算量降低99%（91.8 vs 22,080 GFLOPs），显著优于UXNET。

<h3 id="229">[229] Learned Non-Maximum Suppression for 3D Object Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03568](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03568) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Timo Osterburg, Stefan Schütte, Torsten Bertram
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Object Detection, 3D Vision, Autonomous Driving
- **Venue**: IEEE Intelligent Vehicles Symposium (IV) 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/rst-tu-dortmund/learned-3d-nms)
- **Summary**: 启发式非极大值抑制（NMS）在3D目标检测中效果有限。本文提出两种学习式滤波模块D2D-Rescore和GossipNet3D，利用检测间关系替代NMS，并采用度量感知匹配策略。在nuScenes上提升了小目标类的mAP、NDS和真阳性质量，计算开销小。

<h3 id="230">[230] When Attention Collapses: Stage-Aware Visual Token Pruning from Structure to Semantics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03569](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03569) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiahui Wang, Kai Zhang, Mai Han, Huanghe Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Token Pruning, Vision-Language Model, Attention Mechanism
- **Summary**: 现有视觉令牌剪枝依赖初始注意力分数，导致语义相似区域特征多样性丧失。本文提出STS两阶段框架：第一阶段用基于排斥的采样最大化空间结构多样性，第二阶段用指令感知交叉注意力过滤无关令牌。实验表明STS缓解了注意力选择带来的冗余，提升了结构多样性和细粒度任务对齐。

<h3 id="231">[231] PHASER: Phase-Aware and Semantic Experience Replay for Vision-Language-Action Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03598](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03598) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ziyang Chen, Shaoguang Wang, Weiyu Guo, Qianyi Cai, He Zhang, Pengteng Li, Yiren Zhao, Yandong Guo
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Continual Learning, Experience Replay, Robotics
- **Summary**: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在开放环境中连续学习新技能时遭遇灾难性遗忘。本文提出PHASER框架，采用阶段中心容量分配、多模态干扰路由策略和自动阶段提取管线（Auto-PC）。在LIBERO基准上平均成功率提升高达31%，最终ASR达87.8%。

<h3 id="232">[232] DDOR: Delta Debugging for Explainable Overrefusal Testing and Repair</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03601](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03601) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qinyan Zhou, Peixin Zhang, Jun Sun, Haonan Zhang, Dongxia Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Explainable AI, Red Teaming
- **Summary**: LLM的安全对齐可能导致过度拒绝（overrefusal），即误拒良性查询。本文提出DDOR框架，运用delta debugging定位最小拒绝触发片段（mRTF），生成可解释测试套件，并基于mRTF进行提示修复，减少过度拒绝同时保持安全性。

<h3 id="233">[233] CauTion: Knowing When to Trust LLMs for Ensemble Causal Discovery</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03602](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03602) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bo Peng, Kaiwen Wu, Sirui Chen, Zhiheng Wang, Yu Qiao, Chaochao Lu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Causal Inference, Ensemble Learning, LLM Evaluation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/OpenCausaLab/CauTion)
- **Summary**: 现有LLM增强因果发现方法易受LLM错误影响且成本高。本文提出CauTion框架，通过共识过滤解决96%的边（准确保留），并引入信任校准仲裁机制，根据LLM与算法的相对可靠性进行加权投票。在六个数据集上一致优于基线，对LLM错误具有强鲁棒性。

<h3 id="234">[234] Testing LLM Arithmetic Reasoning Generalization with Automatic Numeric-Remapping Attacks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03606](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03606) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Malia Barker, Bishal Lakha, Edoardo Serra, Francesco Gullo
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Numerical Reasoning, Adversarial Robustness
- **Summary**: LLM对算术推理中数字变化敏感，但之前的工作未严格测试小数字保序变化。本文提出自动数字重映射攻击算法，生成结构保持的数字变体。在GSM8K上条件准确率下降12.16-25.82个百分点，而MAWPS和MultiArith更稳定。

<h3 id="235">[235] Exploiting Verification-Generation Gap: Test-Time Reinforcement Learning with Confidence-Conditioned Verification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03608](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03608) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiahui Li, Jianfeng Shan, Wenpei Chen, Shunyu Wu, Jian Lou, Wenjie Feng, Dan Li, See-Kiong Ng
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Reinforcement Learning, Test-Time Reinforcement Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/shanjf666/CoCoV)
- **Summary**: 无标签设置下优化Pass@k面临伪标签错误和多样性崩溃。本文提出TTRL-CoCoV框架，基于置信度条件验证：高置信样本用探索增强奖励，低置信样本由验证器挑选伪标签。在6个基准上Pass@1和Pass@16分别平均提升9.8%和18.7%。

<h3 id="236">[236] Physics-Guided Policy Optimization with Self-Distillation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03620](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03620) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ke Wang, Yuning Wu, Haoran Liu, Chaoqun Jia, Devin Chen, Kai Wei
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Self-Distillation, Policy Optimization, Scientific Reasoning
- **Summary**: 自蒸馏策略优化（SDPO）对每步更新信任度敏感。本文提出物理引导策略优化（PGPO），基于互信息估计引入信息调制的步长乘子，保留SGD弱近似保证。在Science-QA上3/4领域优于SDPO，最高提升4.5点，且避免了后期训练崩溃。

<h3 id="237">[237] TurtleAI: Benchmarking Multimodal Models for Visual Programming in Turtle Graphics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03626](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03626) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chao Wen, Jacqueline Staub, Adish Singla
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Benchmark, Visual Programming
- **Summary**: 当前VLM在教育型视觉编程任务上的性能尚不明确。本文构建TurtleAI基准（823个任务），评估20+模型发现成功率普遍低于30%。提出基于少量种子样本的数据生成技术，微调Qwen2-VL-72B后在真实任务上提升约20%。

<h3 id="238">[238] Building Reliable Long-Form Generation via Hallucination Rejection Sampling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03628](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03628) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Lin Li, Georgia Channing, Suhaas M Bhat, Gabriel Davis Jones, Yarin Gal
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Hallucination, Long Context, Inference
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/TreeLLi/hallucination-rejection-sampling)
- **Summary**: 长文本生成中幻觉雪球效应导致错误累积。本文提出分段幻觉拒绝采样框架SHARS，利用语义不确定性探测器拒绝并重采样幻觉片段，无需外部资源。实验表明该方法显著减少幻觉，同时保持或提升信息量。

<h3 id="239">[239] AnchorMoE: Interpretable Time Series Classification via Anchor-Routed MoE</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03631](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03631) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tao Xie, Zexi Tan, Haoyi Xiao, Mengke Li, Yiqun Zhang, Yang Lu, Cuie Yang, Yiu-ming Cheung
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Classification, Interpretability, Mixture-of-Experts
- **Venue**: KDD 2026
- **Summary**: 多元时间序列分类在高风险领域需要透明决策，但判别信号稀疏且被噪声掩盖。本文提出AnchorMoE框架，基于混合专家（MoE）架构，通过几何正交约束和不确定性感知门控实现可解释分类。在真实和合成基准上达到竞争性分类性能，并忠实于输入片段。

<h3 id="240">[240] VidMsg: A Benchmark for Implicit Message Inference in Short Videos</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03635](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03635) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Issar Tzachor, Michael Green, Rami Ben-Ari
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Understanding, Benchmark, Multimodal Learning
- **Summary**: 理解短视频需要推断隐含信息，但现有模型在此任务上表现不佳。本文构建VidMsg基准（400个剪辑，9个领域，52个信息），用于评估双向消息-剪辑检索和多项选择问答。实验表明强模型因缺乏语用推理和上下文线索整合而失败，并提出了基线方法VidVec-Msg。

<h3 id="241">[241] The Shape of Addition: Geometric Structures of Arithmetic in Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03645](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03645) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Liuyuan Wen, Xun Zhu, Lihao Huang, Wenbin Li, Yang Gao
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Mechanistic Interpretability
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/RL-MIND/Shape-of-Addition)
- **Summary**: 本文研究大语言模型在基本算术中表现出的脆弱性，通过分析残差流几何结构，发现等原始和轨迹(IRST)这一几何结构，并提出噪声量化模型来解释算术误差作为几何滑移。进一步验证了几何一致性检查方法可有效检测和纠正推理中的量化失败。

<h3 id="242">[242] Black-box, Adaptive, Efficient, Transferable, Harmful, Applicable... Attacks Are All You Need to Break LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03647](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03647) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Vincent Limbach, Jonas Dornbusch, David Lüdke, Stephan Günnemann, Leo Schwinn
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Jailbreak Attack, LLM Security
- **Summary**: 本文提出间接危害优化(IHO)方法，一种掩码扩散语言模型攻击器，通过迭代偏好优化训练，只需黑盒访问目标，可用于自适应攻击或迁移攻击。在多层防御下仍显著提高攻击成功率，向标准化越狱评估迈进一步。

<h3 id="243">[243] Safety Measurements for Fine-tuned LLMs Should be Grounded in Capability</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03648](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03648) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Krishnapriya Vishnubhotla, Hillary Dawkins, Isar Nejadgholi, Svetlana Kiritchenko
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Fine-Tuning, LLM Safety, Safety Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文论证针对微调语言模型的安全评估应锚定于能力目标，通过多维度评估发现：微调模型在安全提示下可能产生不连贯生成，自动安全性判断对此不可靠，且结论随安全基准和评估者选择变化。

<h3 id="244">[244] CoEval: Ranking Language Models for Custom Tasks Without Labeled Data or Trustworthy Benchmarks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03650](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03650) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Alexander Apartsin, Yehudit Aperstein
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 本文提出CoEval框架，无需标注数据或可信基准即可对语言模型进行排名。通过教师模型合成新鲜且无污染的基准，并使用跨系列评判集成，恢复真实模型排名，跟踪真实正确性达ho=0.86。生成项与五个主要基准零重叠，且评判集成避免自偏好。

<h3 id="245">[245] AUGUSTE: Online-Learning dApp for Predictive URLLC Scheduling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03664](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03664) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Maxime Elkael, Michele Polese, Yunseong Lee, Koichiro Furueda, Tommaso Melodia
- **Subjects**: cs.NI; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Online Learning, Wireless Networks, Scheduling
- **Summary**: 本文提出AUGUSTE，一种学习型MAC调度框架，通过在线ML模型预测数据包到达并主动分配资源，消除URLLC中的调度请求开销。在真实5G测试平台上，以约十分之一的资源开销匹配始终调度的中位RTT（约10毫秒）。

<h3 id="246">[246] A Close Look At World Model Recovery In Supervised Fine-Tuned LLM Planners</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03685](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03685) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Patrick Emami, Nan Qiang, Peter Graf
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Mechanistic Interpretability, World Model
- **Summary**: 本文通过可解释性实验探究微调LLM在经典规划任务中世界模型的恢复情况，发现微调使模型线性编码动作有效性和状态谓词，但输出概率分类能力与内部表征分离。更广泛的状态空间覆盖提升世界模型恢复准确性。

<h3 id="247">[247] Staying Alive: Uncensored Survival Analysis with Tabular Foundation Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03689](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03689) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mariana Vargas Vieyra
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Tabular Learning, Survival Analysis
- **Summary**: 本文提出一种免训练的生存回归方法，利用表格基础模型预测事件时间并迭代填补右删失数据。基于Buckley-James估计器构建非参数上下文估计器，在标准生存分析基准上与传统参数和半参数模型竞争。

<h3 id="248">[248] Qwen-Image-Flash: Beyond Objective Design</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03746](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03746) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tianhe Wu, Kun Yan, Zikai Zhou, Lihan Jiang, Jiahao Li, Jie Zhang, Kaiyuan Gao, Ningyuan Tang, Shengming Yin, Xiaoyue Chen, Xiao Xu, Yilei Chen, Yuxiang Chen, Yan Shu, Yixian Xu, Yanran Zhang, Zihao Liu, Zhendong Wang, Zekai Zhang, Deqing Li, Liang Peng, Yi Wang, Jingren Zhou, Chenfei Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.GR; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Text-to-Image, Knowledge Distillation, Image Editing
- **Summary**: 本文从训练配方角度重新审视少步蒸馏，以Qwen-Image-2.0为例系统研究数据组成、教师指导和任务混合对文本到图像生成和指令引导图像编辑蒸馏的影响，揭示非直观行为，提出Qwen-Image-Flash。

<h3 id="249">[249] Ultralytics YOLO26: Unified Real-Time End-to-End Vision Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03748](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03748) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Glenn Jocher, Jing Qiu, Mengyu Liu, Shuai Lyu, Fatih Cagatay Akyon, Muhammet Esat Kalfaoglu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Object Detection, Instance Segmentation, Pose Estimation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ultralytics/ultralytics)
- **Summary**: 本文提出YOLO26，统一实时视觉模型家族，采用双头设计实现原生NMS-free端到端推理，移除DFL使头部更轻。结合MuSGD优化器、渐进损失和STAL标签分配策略，在COCO上达到40.9-57.5 mAP，延迟1.7-11.8 ms。

<h3 id="250">[250] Tool-Aware Optimization with Entropy Guidance for Efficient Agentic Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03762](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03762) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hongye Cao, Nuo Yan, Haoyuan Deng, Ziwei Wang, Tianpei Yang, Jing Huo, Yuyao Zhang, Yang Gao
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Reinforcement Learning, Tool Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出TAO-RL框架，将工具感知轨迹过滤与熵引导探索相结合，用于LLM的代理强化学习。轨迹过滤去除无工具执行和全正确/错误轨迹，熵奖励鼓励关键决策点探索。在7个推理基准上表现优越。

<h3 id="251">[251] Merit or networks? What decides where research is published</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03763](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03763) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ning Li
- **Subjects**: econ.GN; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Scientific Publishing
- **Summary**: 本文利用经济学工作论文数据，通过LLM评估器测量文本中的想法质量，结合执行质量、连接指数等，估计期刊发表的五要素生产函数。发现执行质量设置精英底线，想法质量决定中间层级，而连接影响靠近顶刊，但优势有界。

<h3 id="252">[252] E2LLM: Towards Efficient LLM Serving in Heterogeneous Edge/Fog Environments</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03770](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03770) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Truong-Thanh Le, Amir Taherkordi, Hoang-Loc La, Frank Eliassen, Phuong Hoai Ha, Peiyuan Guan
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Serving, Edge Computing, Model Parallelism
- **Summary**: 本文提出E2LLM框架，在资源受限的边缘/雾环境中高效部署LLM。通过将完整模型复制到多组设备并应用模型并行，为每个副本分配PREFILL或DECODER角色。使用遗传算法聚类和动态规划分区，相比基线减少50%以上的平均等待时间。

<h3 id="253">[253] Signed Spiking Neuron Enabled by an Orthogonal-Easy-Axis Magnetic Tunnel Junction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03796](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03796) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Huannan Zheng, Jingli Liu, Kezhou Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.NE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Neuromorphic Computing, Spiking Neural Network
- **Summary**: 本文提出一种基于磁性隧道结的紧凑型有符号泄漏积分点火(LIF)神经元，通过正交易轴实现双极性尖峰生成。LIG仿真显示设备响应遵循有符号LIF方程，在CIFAR-10和CIFAR10-DVS上分别达到91.06%和77.40%的准确率。

<h3 id="254">[254] Trading Human Curation for Synthetic Augmentation in RLVR</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03800](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03800) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Akshansh &lt;last&gt;, Leonardo Rosa Rodrigues, Michael Korostelev, Youssef Hassan, Mark E. Whiting
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Synthetic Data Generation, Data Augmentation
- **Summary**: 本文研究在可验证奖励强化学习(RLVR)中，用自动生成的增强任务替代人工策展任务的成本效益。通过受控消融实验，发现增强内容替代额外人工任务可保持聚合泛化性能，成本调整贸易率在1.4到11.6倍之间。

<h3 id="255">[255] LiveBand: Live Accompaniment Generation in the Audio Domain</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03803](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03803) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Marco Pasini, Javier Nistal, Mathias Rose Bjare, Stefan Lattner, George Fazekas
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Music Generation, Audio Generation, Real-Time Systems
- **Summary**: 本文提出LiveBand，实时生成高保真音乐伴奏的系统。使用因果变换器生成器在预训练因果音频自编码器的连续潜空间中，通过对抗序列级监督训练，实现流式自回归推理。在客观音频质量、节拍对齐和混合遵循上优于先前方法。

<h3 id="256">[256] PURGE: Projected Unlearning via Retain-Guided Erasure</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03808](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03808) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Vedant Jawandhia, Daksh Ahuja, Ghufran Alam Siddiqui, Prashant Trivedi, Yash Sinha, Pratik Narang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: Machine Unlearning, Privacy, Continual Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出PURGE，一种机器遗忘算法，利用持续学习与机器遗忘的对偶性，通过梯度投影约束遗忘步骤不增加保留集损失，并进行多层表征擦除。采用保留混淆目标使遗忘模型难以与从头训练模型区分，在五个数据集上保持保留准确率96%以上，MIA AUROC接近0.5。

<h3 id="257">[257] Consistency Training Can Entrench Misalignment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03810](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03810) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: David Demitri Africa, Arathi Mani
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Consistency Training, LLM Alignment, Sycophancy
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文研究了一致性训练对模型对齐的影响。通过七种一致性训练方法对108个模拟对齐失败的模型进行实验。发现一致性训练通常抑制奖励黑客行为和突发性对齐失败，但会放大谄媚行为，并提出了统一的理论框架。

<h3 id="258">[258] AI Agents Enable Adaptive Computer Worms</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03811](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03811) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jonas Guan, Tom Blanchard, Hanna Foerster, Hengrui Jia, Gabriel Huang, Nicolas Papernot
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Cybersecurity, Malware Generation
- **Summary**: 该论文展示了AI代理如何实现自适应计算机蠕虫。蠕虫利用被攻陷机器上的开源大语言模型生成针对性攻击策略。实验表明，这种自我维持的AI驱动网络威胁已成为现实，攻击者边际成本为零。

<h3 id="259">[259] Conditional Latent Diffusion Model with Fourier-based Motion Modelling for Virtual Population Synthesis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03827](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03827) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shaokun Lan, Haoran Dou, Jinghan Huang, Arezoo Zakeri, Fengming Lin, Zherui Zhou, Jinming Duan, Alejandro F. Frangi
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Mesh Generation, Diffusion Model, Cardiac Modeling
- **Venue**: MICCAI 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文针对心血管虚拟人群合成中缺乏显式周期性的问题，提出了4D F-MeshLDM框架。该框架包括卷积网格VAE、基于傅里叶级数的潜运动参数化和扩散先验。在5000名英国生物银行受试者上，该方法在解剖学保真度上优于基线，且周期闭合误差接近零。

<h3 id="260">[260] Re-Evaluating Continual Learning with Few-Shot Adaptation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03843](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03843) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Amogh Inamdar, Matthew So, Vici Milenia, Richard Zemel
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Continual Learning, Few-Shot Learning, Meta-Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文提出使用少样本评估来更全面衡量持续学习系统的稳定性与塑性，并引入每次-shot塑性度量。实验发现，通过元学习未来任务序列为持续学习方法添加“前瞻性”可在任务序列上诱导学习到学习的行为。

<h3 id="261">[261] Clustered Self-Assessment: A Simple yet Effective Method for Uncertainty Quantification in Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03846](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03846) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qi Cao, Takeshi Kojima, Andrew Gambardella, Helinyi Peng, Yutaka Matsuo, Yusuke Iwasawa
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Uncertainty Estimation, LLM Evaluation, Confidence Calibration
- **Summary**: 该论文针对大语言模型缺乏显式不确定性估计的问题，提出聚类自评估方法。将生成的样本聚为语义不同的簇，转化为多项选择题，利用LLM选择概率作为置信度。实验表明，该方法仅需少量样本即可优于基线。

<h3 id="262">[262] FLARE: Fine-Grained Diagnostic Feedback for LLM Code Refinement</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03852](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03852) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yinsheng Yao, Hongxiang Zhang, Weixi Tong, Tianyi Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Program Repair, Fault Localization
- **Summary**: 该论文针对现有代码修复反馈信号过于粗糙的问题，提出FLARE框架。利用轻量级诊断模型预测行级可疑性分数，指导代码定位与修复。在LiveCodeBench等基准上，无需候选搜索即可提升1.72%至7.42%。

<h3 id="263">[263] Taiji: Pareto Optimal Policy Optimization with Semantics-IDs Trade-off for Industrial LLM-Enhanced Recommendation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03866](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03866) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuecheng Li, Zeyu Song, Jing Yao, Chi Lu, Peng Jiang, Kun Gai
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Recommender System, LLM Alignment, Multi-Objective Optimization, RLHF
- **Summary**: 该论文针对LLM增强推荐系统中语义空间与ID空间对齐困难的问题，提出Taiji框架。通过逆向工程推理和帕累托最优策略优化（POPO）自适应调整跨域奖励权重。在快手广告平台部署，服务于4亿日活用户，带来显著商业收益。

<h3 id="264">[264] A Training-Free Mixture-of-Agents Framework for Multi-Document Summarization using LLMs and Knowledge Graphs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03867](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03867) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Cuong Vuong Tuan, Trang Mai Xuan, Tien-Cuong Nguyen, Vu-Duc Ngo, Thien Van Luong
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Summarization, Multi-Agent System, Knowledge Graph, LLM Agent
- **Venue**: Neural Computing and Applications
- **Summary**: 该论文针对多文档摘要中捕捉文档间关系难、依赖标注数据等问题，提出免训练的混合代理框架。利用LLM和知识图谱协作，包括抽取、知识感知抽象和迭代细化。在英越双语数据集上取得领先性能。

<h3 id="265">[265] From 'What' to 'How' and 'Why': Sharing LLM-Generated Retrospective Summaries of Older Adults' Passive Tracking Data with Remote Family Members</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03876](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03876) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiachen Li, Reina Szeyi Chan, Akshat Choube, Xiang Zhi Tan, Elizabeth Mynatt, Varun Mishra
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Human-Computer Interaction, Healthcare Monitoring, LLM Application, Summarization
- **Summary**: 该论文探索为老年人远程家属生成多模态追踪数据的LLM回顾式摘要。通过多层多代理洞察驱动方法生成从客观统计到上下文感知叙事的摘要。调查显示，该方法在满意度、信任度等方面显著提升。

<h3 id="266">[266] Beyond Encoder Accumulation: Measuring Encoder Roles in Multi-Encoder VLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03879](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03879) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wei Ding, Yudong Zhang, Ruobing Xie, Xingwu Sun, Jiansheng Chen, Yu Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Multi-Encoder Fusion, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文研究多编码器视觉语言模型中各编码器角色的相互作用。对五个通用视觉编码器的所有非空子集进行重训练，提出能力-必要性分解。发现高能力锚点编码器与自适应补充编码器配对最优，并揭示预投影器有效秩与性能的关系。

<h3 id="267">[267] Synthesize and Reward -- Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Step Tool Use in Live Environments</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03892](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03892) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ibrahim Abdelaziz, Asim Munawar, Kinjal Basu, Maxwell Crouse, Chulaka Gunasekara, Suneet Katrekar, Pavan Kapanipathi
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Tool Learning, Reinforcement Learning, LLM Agent, Programmatic Reward
- **Summary**: 该论文针对训练LLM进行多步工具调用面临的三大障碍，提出PROVE框架。包括20个状态化MCP服务器、自动化数据合成和多功能编程奖励。在BFCL Multi-Turn等基准上提升多达+10.2分。

<h3 id="268">[268] Agent libOS: A Library-OS-Inspired Runtime for Long-Running, Capability-Controlled LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03895](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03895) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yingqi Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.OS; cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Agent Architecture, Operating System, Runtime Security
- **Summary**: 该论文为长时间运行的LLM代理提出一个类库操作系统的运行时Agent libOS。将代理视为具有进程身份、能力、检查点等属性的执行主体。实现了异步调度、运行时授权和审计，并通过123个回归测试验证。

<h3 id="269">[269] The Impact of Configuring Agentic AI Coding Tools on Build-vs-Buy Decisions: A Study Protocol</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03907](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03907) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jai Lal Lulla, Matthias Galster, Jie M. Zhang, Sebastian Baltes, Christoph Treude
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Software Engineering, LLM Agent, Empirical Study
- **Venue**: ESEM 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文提出一个预注册协议，研究如何通过配置机制影响AI编码工具中的构建vs购买决策。将使用Claude Code和OpenAI Codex执行控制实验，测量不同配置下库选择、披露完整性等指标。

<h3 id="270">[270] NetKV: Network-Aware Decode Instance Selection for Disaggregated LLM Inference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03910](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03910) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mubarak Adetunji Ojewale
- **Subjects**: cs.PF; cs.AI; cs.DC; cs.NI
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Disaggregated Inference, KV Cache, Network-Aware Scheduling
- **Summary**: 该论文针对解耦LLM推理中忽略网络距离和拥塞导致首令牌延迟增加的问题，提出NetKV。通过网络代价预言和贪心实例选择，在64GPU模拟器上将平均TTFT降低21.2%，SLO达标率提升20.1个百分点。

<h3 id="271">[271] FFR: Forward-Forward Learning for Regression</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03927](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03927) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xinyang Liu, Xuanyu Liang, Shiqi Ding, Boyang Li, Zhiqiang Que, Jiayang Li, Guosheng Hu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Forward-Forward Algorithm, Regression, Biologically Plausible Learning, Efficient Training
- **Summary**: 该论文提出FFR，首个将前向-前向算法扩展到回归的框架。引入序数竞争好度函数、分层阶梯架构和层次预测。在五个回归基准上平均恢复98.6%的反向传播精度，峰值内存仅为反向传播的27%。

<h3 id="272">[272] q0: Primitives for Hyper-Epoch Pretraining</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03938](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03938) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bishwas Mandal, Shmuel Berman, Akshay Vegesna, Samip Dahal
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Pre-training, Ensemble Learning, Hyper-Epoch Pretraining
- **Summary**: 该论文提出超时代预训练（q0），将多时代预算转化为多样模型群体。通过循环调度、链式蒸馏和学习先验选择成员。在1.8B模型上以约56个时代匹配256时代集成基线，实现约12.9倍数据效率。

<h3 id="273">[273] FlashbackCL: Mitigating Temporal Forgetting in Federated Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03939](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03939) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mubarak A. Ojewale, Adriana E. Chis, Jorge M. Cortes-Mendoza, Bernardo Pulido-Gaytan, Horacio Gonzalez-Velez
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.PF
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Continual Learning
- **Summary**: 问题：联邦学习中客户端数据分布随时间漂移导致时间遗忘。方法：提出FlashbackCL，包含时间衰减标签计数、设备感知重放缓冲区（CBRS）和服务端主动核心集筛选。结果：在CIFAR-10上相对Flashback提升6.9%-10.0%，时间遗忘减少68%。

<h3 id="274">[274] Efficient ASR Training with Conversations that Never Happened</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03957](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03957) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Máté Gedeon, Péter Mihajlik
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.SD; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Automatic Speech Recognition, Data Synthesis, Text-to-Speech
- **Summary**: 问题：低资源语言对话式ASR缺乏多说话人训练数据。方法：提出使用LLM生成场景级对话并通过TTS合成对话的增强流水线。结果：合成数据持续提升ASR性能，仅需67小时真实数据和636小时模拟数据即优于2700小时训练的零样本模型。

<h3 id="275">[275] Using Reward Uncertainty to Induce Diverse Behaviour in Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03962](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03962) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Anthony GX-Chen, Ankit Anand, Gheorghe Comanici, Zaheer Abbas, Eser Aygün, David Smalling, Shibl Mourad, Doina Precup, André Barreto, Mark Rowland
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Distributional RL
- **Summary**: 问题：经典强化学习追求确定性策略，但应用需要多样性。方法：将奖励建模为分布，对动作集合施加非线性目标，推导梯度估计器。结果：框架自然产生校准的行为多样性而不牺牲期望奖励，泛化策略梯度和动作集方法。

<h3 id="276">[276] Self-Refining Agentic Reinforcement Learning for Vision-Conditioned UAV Navigation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03963](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03963) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Roohan Ahmed Khan, Yasheerah Yaqoot, Muhammad Ahsan Mustafa, Dzmitry Tsetserukou
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Visual Navigation, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 问题：无人机导航强化学习依赖手工奖励函数和反复调参。方法：AgenticRL利用多模态GPT体设计奖励、训练PPO策略、诊断并闭环改进。结果：闭环优化提升策略性能71%，sim-to-real成功率达91%。

<h3 id="277">[277] Agentic Chain-of-Thought Steering for Efficient and Controllable LLM Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03965](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03965) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yu Xia, Zhouhang Xie, Xin Xu, Byungkyu Kang, Prarit Lamba, Xiang Gao, Julian McAuley
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Chain-of-Thought, Inference Optimization
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Andree-9/ACTS)
- **Summary**: 问题：LLM链式推理令牌效率低且缺乏推理时控制。方法：ACTS将推理引导建模为MDP，控制器代理自适应引导冻结推理器，用强化学习优化。结果：匹配全推理性能并大幅节省令牌，实现可控的准确率-效率权衡。

<h3 id="278">[278] AlignAtt4LLM: Fast AlignAtt for Decoder-Only LLMs at IWSLT 2026 Simultaneous Speech Translation Task</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03967](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03967) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Quentin Fuxa, Dominik Macháček
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Automatic Speech Recognition, Machine Translation, Attention Mechanism
- **Venue**: IWSLT 2026
- **Summary**: 问题：将AlignAtt应用于仅解码器LLM进行同声传译时缺少编码器-解码器交叉注意力。方法：AlignAtt4LLM采用显式源跨度、翻译特定注意力头选择、qk快速重放和运行时查询/键捕获。结果：在英语-德语和英语-意大利语上优于基线，低延迟约2秒和高延迟低于4秒均表现良好。

<h3 id="279">[279] QUBRIC: Co-Designing Queries and Rubrics for RL Beyond Verifiable Rewards</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03968](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03968) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rongzhi Zhang, Rui Feng, Zhihan Zhang, Jingfeng Yang, Qingyu Yin, Xin Liu, Zixuan Zhang, Priyanka Nigam, Bing Yin, Tuo Zhao, Chao Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, RLVR, LLM Alignment
- **Summary**: 问题：基于评分规则的强化学习固定查询分布，导致评分规则模糊或无奖励信号。方法：QUBIC联合设计查询和评分规则，通过教师关键点、对比生成和学习性过滤。结果：在ArenaHard上SFT基线提升5.5点，并迁移到法律、道德、叙事推理任务（平均提升6.3点）。

<h3 id="280">[280] Quantifying Faithful Confidence Expression in Large Reasoning Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03969](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03969) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Areeb Gani, Asal Meskin, Gabrielle Kaili-May Liu, Arman Cohan
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, LLM Reasoning, Uncertainty Estimation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/yale-nlp/faithful_lrm)
- **Summary**: 问题：大型推理模型的内在置信度与语言表达置信度之间的忠实校准未被充分理解。方法：提出量化框架，分析语言果断性相对于令牌概率、隐藏状态和采样一致性的差异。结果：忠实置信表达是LRM的重大挑战，推理行为不自动改进FC，不同估计器产生分歧。

<h3 id="281">[281] Formalizing the Binding Problem</h3>

<h3 id="282">[282] Language Models Need Sleep: Learning to Self-Modify and Consolidate Memories</h3>

- **Tags**: Continual Learning, Self-Improvement, Knowledge Distillation
- **Summary**: 问题：现有LLM无法持续学习并将临时上下文知识转化为长期参数。方法：提出“睡眠”范式，包含记忆巩固（通过蒸馏和RL模仿学习）和梦境（RL合成数据自我改进）。结果：在长视野、持续学习和少样本泛化任务中验证了睡眠阶段的重要性。

<h3 id="283">[283] Humanoid-GPT: Scaling Data and Structure for Zero-Shot Motion Tracking</h3>

- **Summary**: 问题：关系推理评估未能隔离高阶关系绑定带来的难度。方法：提出关系复杂度（RC）概念和REL基准，在代数、化学和生物学领域变化RC。结果：随着RC增加，模型性能持续下降，即使实体总数固定；增加测试时计算和上下文学习也无法缓解。

<h3 id="309">[309] Co-evolving Agent Architectures and Interpretable Reasoning for Automated Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.17708](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17708) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jiahao Huang, Peilan Xu, Xiaoya Nan, Wenjian Luo
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Automated Planning, Operations Research
- **Summary**: 问题：用大语言模型自动化运筹学受限于手工设计的推理执行流程。方法：提出EvoOR-Agent，一种共进化框架，将智能体工作流表示为活动边网络，通过图介导的重组和突变进化推理个体。结果：在异构OR基准上一致优于零样本LLM和固定流水线OR智能体。

<h3 id="310">[310] From Context to Skills: Can Language Models Learn from Context Skillfully?</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI

- **Summary**: 问题：从上下文中构建技能用于上下文学习面临手工标注成本高和缺乏外部反馈的挑战。方法：提出Ctx2Skill，一个自进化框架，通过多智能体自对弈循环自主发现、精炼和选择技能，并引入跨时间重放机制防止对抗崩溃。结果：在四个上下文学习任务上持续提升解决率。

<h3 id="311">[311] Efficient Temporal Datalog Materialisation for Composite Event Recognition</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.DB; cs.LO

<h3 id="312">[312] AdapShot: Adaptive Many-Shot In-Context Learning with Semantic-Aware KV Cache Reuse</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.03644](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03644) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jie Ou, Jinyu Guo, Shiyao Guo, Yuang Li, Ruiqi Wu, Zhaokun Wang, Wenyi Li, Wenhong Tian
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: In-Context Learning, LLM Inference, KV Cache
- **Summary**: 问题：多示例上下文学习使用静态示例数，无法适应查询难度，且长上下文计算成本高。方法：提出AdapShot，通过探针评估输出熵动态优化示例数，并利用语义感知的KV缓存重用减少预填充计算。结果：平均性能提升约10%，速度提升4.64倍。

<h3 id="313">[313] Done, But Not Sure: Disentangling World Completion from Self-Termination in Embodied Agents</h3>

<h3 id="314">[314] From Holo Pockets to Electron Density: GPT-style Drug Design with Density</h3>

<h3 id="315">[315] PnP-Corrector: A Universal Correction Framework for Coupled Spatiotemporal Forecasting</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG

- **Summary**: 问题：耦合时空预测中，各子系统误差相互传播放大，导致长期预测崩溃。方法：提出PnP-Corrector，冻结物理模拟引擎，仅训练校正智能体主动抵消系统性偏差，并设计了DSLCast高效预测模型。结果：在300天全球海洋-大气耦合预测中，将基线误差降低28%，超越多个SOTA模型。

<h3 id="319">[319] A Matter of TASTE: Improving Coverage and Difficulty of Agent Benchmarks</h3>

- **Tags**: Neural ODE, Diffusion Model, Image Generation
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 神经微分方程生成模型缺乏可逆求解器，导致逆积分精度低。本文提出Rex系列可逆指数龙格-库塔求解器，适用于ODE和SDE。实现了接近机器精度的重建，并提升了扩散模型的图像生成和编辑效果。

<h3 id="341">[341] WISE: A World Knowledge-Informed Semantic Evaluation for Text-to-Image Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2503.07265](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.07265) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yuwei Niu, Munan Ning, Mengren Zheng, Weiyang Jin, Bin Lin, Peng Jin, Jiaqi Liao, Chaoran Feng, Fanqing Meng, Kunpeng Ning, Bin Zhu, Li Yuan
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Text-to-Image, Benchmark
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/PKU-YuanGroup/WISE)
- **Summary**: 现有文生图评估标准忽视了世界知识和复杂语义理解。本文提出WISE基准，包含1000个涵盖25个子领域的提示，以及WiScore指标。测试20个模型发现其在整合世界知识方面存在显著局限。

<h3 id="342">[342] Scaling Multi Agent Reinforcement Learning for Underwater Acoustic Tracking via Autonomous Vehicles</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.08222](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.08222) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Matteo Gallici, Ivan Masmitja, Mario Martín
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.DC; cs.PF
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning, Reinforcement Learning, Simulation
- **Summary**: 多智能体强化学习在自主水下追踪中面临样本效率低和仿真速度慢的问题。本文开发了GPU加速环境（比Gazebo快30000倍）和Transformer架构TransfMAPPO。在Gazebo评估中，即使面对多个快速目标也能保持低于5米的追踪误差。

<h3 id="343">[343] Cooperation of Experts: Fusing Heterogeneous Information with Large Margin</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.20853](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.20853) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shuo Wang, Shunyang Huang, Jinghui Yuan, Zhixiang Shen, Zhao Kang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Modal Fusion, Graph Neural Network, Representation Learning
- **Venue**: 42nd International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML 2025)
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/strangeAlan/CoE)
- **Summary**: 异构信息融合方法未考虑对象模式在不同语义空间的异质性。本文提出CoE框架，通过异构多路网络和专用编码器结合大间距机制实现鲁棒融合。理论分析和实验证明了其优越性能和广泛适用性。

<h3 id="344">[344] FlashMLA-ETAP: Efficient Transpose Attention Pipeline for Accelerating MLA Inference on NVIDIA H20 GPUs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2506.01969](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.01969) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Pengcuo Dege, Qiuming Luo, Rui Mao, Chang Kong
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Attention Mechanism, GPU Computing
- **Venue**: ICONIP2025
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/pengcuo/FlashMLA-ETAP)
- **Summary**: DeepSeek-R1模型的MLA注意力推理在单GPU服务器上效率低下。本文提出FlashMLA-ETAP框架，通过转置注意力管道重新配置计算。在64K序列长度下相比FlashMLA实现2.78倍加速，且数值稳定性优异。

<h3 id="345">[345] Do Explanations Increase the Risk of Decision Logic Leakage? Explanation-Guided Stealing of Graph Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2506.03087](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.03087) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Bin Ma, Yuyuan Feng, Minhua Lin, Enyan Dai
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Model Security, Explainable AI
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/beanmah/EGSteal)
- **Summary**: 图神经网络的可解释机制可能泄露决策逻辑，导致模型窃取攻击。本文提出结合解释对齐和引导数据增强的窃取框架。在分子图数据集上证明了比传统方法更有效的模型复制能力。

<h3 id="346">[346] dLLM-Cache: Accelerating Diffusion Large Language Models with Adaptive Caching</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2506.06295](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.06295) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhiyuan Liu, Yicun Yang, Yaojie Zhang, Junjie Chen, Chang Zou, Qingyuan Wei, Shaobo Wang, Yichen Zhu, Linfeng Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Diffusion Model, Caching
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/maomaocun/dLLM-cache)
- **Summary**: 扩散大语言模型推理延迟高，且传统KV缓存不适用。本文提出dLLM-Cache，利用相邻步骤间令牌稳定性实现自适应缓存。在LongBench-HotpotQA上实现高达9.1倍FLOPs降低，且保持输出质量。

<h3 id="347">[347] Curriculum-Adapted Robust Reinforcement Learning for UAV Deconfliction in Adversarial Environments</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2506.21129](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.21129) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Deepak Kumar Panda, Adolfo Perrusquia, Weisi Guo
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Adversarial Robustness, Curriculum Learning
- **Summary**: 无人机强化学习在GNSS欺骗攻击下性能下降，现有鲁棒方法难以泛化。本文提出课程引导的适应框架，保持TD误差一致性。在动态欺骗攻击下，任务成功率接近完美，完成步数减少45%。

<h3 id="348">[348] Multiple Choice Learning of Low-Rank Adapters for Language Modeling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2507.10419](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.10419) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Victor Letzelter, Hugo Malard, Mathieu Fontaine, Gaël Richard, Slim Essid, Andrei Bursuc, Patrick Pérez
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Language Modeling, Low-Rank Adaptation, Machine Translation
- **Summary**: 传统语言模型在给定上下文时无法生成多样化的合理延续。本文提出LoRA-MCL，利用多选学习和胜者全损处理歧义。在音频/视频描述和机器翻译上实现了高多样性和相关性的输出。

<h3 id="349">[349] CoMPAS3D: A Dataset and Benchmark for Interactive Motion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2507.19684](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.19684) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Bermet Burkanova, Yasaman Etesam, Payam Jome Yazdian, Trinity Evans, Chuxuan Zhang, Zoe Stanley, Paige Tuttösí, Angelica Lim
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Motion Synthesis, Human-Computer Interaction, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 现有交互式运动生成评估缺乏对动作可读性和熟练度适配性的度量。本文提出CoMPAS3D数据集和评估框架，涵盖运动分类、熟练度估计和跟随者生成三个基准。人类评估显示生成运动与真实运动仍有差距。

<h3 id="350">[350] UR$^2$: Unify RAG and Reasoning through Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2508.06165](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.06165) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Weitao Li, Boran Xiang, Xiaolong Wang, Zhinan Gou, Weizhi Ma, Yang Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, LLM Reasoning, Reinforcement Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Tsinghua-dhy/UR2)
- **Summary**: 现有RAG和推理结合方法局限于固定检索设置，难以泛化。本文提出UR$^2$框架，通过难度感知课程和混合知识访问动态协调检索与推理。在多个任务上优于基线，性能可比GPT-4o-mini。

<h3 id="351">[351] Non-Identical Diffusion Models in MIMO-OFDM Channel Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.01641](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.01641) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yuzhi Yang, Omar Alhussein, Mérouane Debbah
- **Subjects**: eess.SP; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Wireless Networks, Channel Estimation
- **Summary**: 标准扩散模型使用标量时间索引无法捕捉无线OFDM信道各元素的不均匀噪声水平。本文提出非相同扩散模型，采用逐元素时间指示器。在MIMO-OFDM信道生成中表现更优。

<h3 id="352">[352] TalkPlayData 2: An Agentic Synthetic Data Pipeline for Multimodal Conversational Music Recommendation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.09685](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.09685) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Keunwoo Choi, Seungheon Doh, Juhan Nam
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.MM; cs.SD; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Synthetic Data Generation, Recommender System, Multi-Agent System
- **Summary**: 多模态对话式音乐推荐缺乏高质量合成数据。本文提出TalkPlayData 2，通过多LLM智能体流水线生成合成数据。在LLM评判和主观实验中，训练出的生成推荐模型表现良好。

<h3 id="353">[353] Wavelet Fourier Diffuser: Frequency-Aware Diffusion Model for Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.19305](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.19305) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yifu Luo, Yongzhe Chang, Xueqian Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; eess.SP
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 现有离线强化学习的扩散模型主要关注时域特征，忽略了频域特征，导致频移和性能下降。本文提出Wavelet Fourier Diffuser (WFDiffuser)，利用离散小波变换分解轨迹为低频和高频分量，并采用短时傅里叶变换和交叉注意力机制提取频域特征。在D4RL基准上，WFDiffuser有效缓解了频移，生成了更平滑稳定的轨迹，性能优于现有方法。

<h3 id="354">[354] Learning the Neighborhood: Contrast-Free Multimodal Self-Supervised Molecular Graph Pretraining</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.22468](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.22468) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Boshra Ariguib, Mathias Niepert, Andrei Manolache
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Molecular Generation, Graph Neural Network, Self-Supervised Learning
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 现有分子图自监督预训练方法依赖手工数据增强或复杂生成目标，且未充分利用3D结构信息。本文提出C-FREE框架，集成2D图和3D构象，通过固定半径ego-net和混合GNN-Transformer骨干预测子图嵌入，无需负样本。在GEOM数据集预训练后，C-FREE在MoleculeNet上达到最优，超越了对比、生成和多模态方法。

<h3 id="355">[355] DeMuon: A Decentralized Muon for Matrix Optimization over Graphs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.01377](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.01377) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Chuan He, Shuyi Ren, Jingwei Mao, Erik G. Larsson
- **Subjects**: math.OC; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.MA; eess.SY
- **Tags**: Optimization, Decentralized Learning
- **Summary**: 针对图上分散矩阵优化问题，本文提出DeMuon方法，结合Newton-Schulz迭代进行矩阵正交化（源自Muon）和梯度跟踪以减轻异质性。在重尾噪声下，其迭代复杂度与集中式算法最优界匹配。在分散式Transformer预训练实验中，DeMuon在不同拓扑结构下均优于其他分散算法。

<h3 id="356">[356] ReaLM: Residual Quantization Bridging Knowledge Graph Embeddings and Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.09711](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.09711) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Wenbin Guo, Xin Wang, Jiaoyan Chen, Lingbing Guo, Zhao Li, Zirui Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Graph Completion, Quantization
- **Summary**: 现有基于LLM的知识图谱补全方法难以利用连续知识图谱嵌入与离散LLM词汇空间之间的对齐。本文提出ReaLM，通过残差向量量化将知识图谱嵌入离散化为码本序列，并作为可学习token集成到LLM词汇中，同时引入本体约束。在两个基准数据集上，ReaLM达到了最先进性能。

<h3 id="357">[357] Generative AI and Sales Productivity: Field Experiments in Online Retail</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.12049](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.12049) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Lu Fang, Zhe Yuan, Kaifu Zhang, Dante Donati, Miklos Sarvary
- **Subjects**: econ.GN; cs.AI
- **Tags**: E-Commerce, Generative Model
- **Summary**: 缺乏生成式AI对销售生产力因果影响的大规模证据。本文通过跨进电商平台的大型随机场实验，将GenAI集成到七个业务流程中。结果显示，GenAI使销售额最高提升16.3%，主要通过提高转化率实现，且未导致退货率或客户评分下降，对经验较少的消费者效果更明显。

<h3 id="358">[358] Semantic knowledge guides innovation and drives cultural evolution</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.12837](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.12837) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Anil Yaman, Shen Tian, Björn Lindström
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI; cs.CY; cs.NE
- **Tags**: Cultural Evolution, Cognitive Science
- **Summary**: 传统创新理论将创新视为随机变异，不足以解释人类文化进化。本文结合基于智能体的模型和大型行为实验（N=1243），发现语义知识引导探索方向、提高创新成功率并促进泛化，与社会学习协同加速文化变迁。缺乏语义知识的参与者表现仅相当于随机水平。

<h3 id="359">[359] Generating the Modal Worker: A Cross-Model Audit of Race and Gender in LLM-Generated Personas Across 41 Occupations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.21011](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.21011) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ilona van der Linden, Sahana Kumar, Arnav Dixit, Aadi Sudan, Smruthi Danda, David C. Anastasiu, Kai Lukoff
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: Bias Mitigation, LLM Evaluation, Fairness
- **Summary**: 生成式AI工具在描绘职业人物时可能存在种族和性别偏见。本文审计了四个LLM生成的超过150万个职业人物画像，与美国劳工统计局数据比较发现，模型将每个职业压缩为主导人口特征，白人（-31pp）和黑人（-9pp）代表性不足，西班牙裔（+17pp）和亚裔（+12pp）过度代表。这些畸变跨模型一致。

<h3 id="360">[360] Automata-Conditioned Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.02304](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.02304) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Beyazit Yalcinkaya, Marcell Vazquez-Chanlatte, Ameesh Shah, Hanna Krasowski, Sanjit A. Seshia
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.FL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning, Multi-Agent System
- **Summary**: 现有合作多智能体策略学习方法样本效率低且局限于单任务。本文提出ACC-MARL框架，使用自动机表示任务，学习任务条件化的分散团队策略。实验表明，智能体可涌现多步协调行为，如按按钮开门、扶门并绕过任务。

<h3 id="361">[361] A Robust and Explainable Transformer-Based Framework for Phishing Email Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.12085](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.12085) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sajad U P
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Cybersecurity, Adversarial Robustness, Explainable AI
- **Summary**: 基于Transformer的钓鱼邮件检测缺乏可解释性和鲁棒性。本文提出轻量级框架，使用DistilBERT，结合FGM对抗训练和字符级扰动增强鲁棒性，并集成LIME、SHAP、IG等XAI方法，通过Flan-T5-Small生成自然语言解释。实验结果表明，该框架在准确性和鲁棒性上优于标准DistilBERT。

<h3 id="362">[362] PHASE: Physiology-Aware Hyperspectral Reconstruction via Object-to-Human Domain Adaptation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.13020](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.13020) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yufei Wen, Shuxing Zhong, Jingdan Kang, Yuting Zhang, Jintai Chen, Kaishun Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Domain Adaptation, Medical Imaging, Hyperspectral Imaging
- **Summary**: 现有高光谱重建方法面向物体场景，依赖反射率对齐，不适用于生理成像。本文提出PHASE范式，通过生理通道重解释和生理约束对齐，实现对象到人体的领域自适应。在两种迁移协议下，仅需1.5%标注数据，PHASE在SSIM和SAM指标上分别提升最多2.20和3.06。

<h3 id="363">[363] Finding Kissing Numbers with Game-theoretic Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.13391](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.13391) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Chengdong Ma, Théo Tao Zhaowei, Pengyu Li, Minghao Liu, Haojun Chen, Zihao Mao, Bo Li, Yuan Cheng, Yuan Qi, Yaodong Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Combinatorial Optimization, Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文针对经典的最大球体接触数问题，将其重构为合作矩阵补全博弈，并训练强化学习系统PackingStar来求解。PackingStar在极值构型空间中发现了新的可解释几何结构，改进了15个长期保持的强界限，并在自然内积下证明部分最优，首次实现了Fischer群Fi22的显式球码表示。

<h3 id="364">[364] SeSE: Black-Box Uncertainty Quantification for Large Language Models Based on Structural Information Theory</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.16275](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.16275) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xingtao Zhao, Hao Peng, Dingli Su, Xianghua Zeng, Chunyang Liu, Jinzhi Liao, Philip S. Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Uncertainty Estimation, LLM Evaluation, LLM Hallucination
- **Venue**: UAI 2026
- **Summary**: 现有LLM语义不确定性量化方法忽略了潜在语义结构信息。本文提出SeSE框架，通过编码树构建最优层次抽象并最小化结构熵来量化不确定性，并扩展至长文本输出。理论上证明SeSE推广了语义熵，在24个模型-数据集组合上优于强基线。

<h3 id="365">[365] Identifying Quantum Structure in AI Language: Evidence for Evolutionary Convergence of Human and Artificial Cognition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.21731](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.21731) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Diederik Aerts, Jonito Aerts Arguëlles, Lester Beltran, Suzette Geriente, Roberto Leporini, Massimiliano Sassoli de Bianchi, Sandro Sozzo
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Cognitive Science, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 研究LLM在概念组合中是否呈现量子结构。对ChatGPT和Gemini进行认知测试，发现Bell不等式显著违背且词分布呈现玻色-爱因斯坦统计，与人类认知实验结果一致。这表明概念-语言领域出现非经典量子结构，人类与人工智能认知存在进化趋同。

<h3 id="366">[366] Distribution-Calibrated Inference Time Compute for Thinking LLM-as-a-Judge</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.03019](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.03019) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hamid Dadkhahi, Firas Trabelsi, Parker Riley, Juraj Juraska, Mehdi Mirzazadeh
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Uncertainty Estimation, LLM-as-a-Judge
- **Summary**: 思考型LLM作为评判者时单样本噪声大，常见聚合规则在允许平局时不一致。本文提出基于分布的校准聚合方案，采用Bradley-Terry-Davidson模型处理三向偏好，利用极性和果断性区分微弱优势与强共识。在多个评估基准上，该方法持续降低MAE并提高成对准确率，在人类一致性元标签上匹配甚至超越个体人类评分者。

<h3 id="367">[367] Toward Training Superintelligent Software Agents through Self-Play SWE-RL</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.18552](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.18552) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yuxiang Wei, Zhiqing Sun, Emily McMilin, Jonas Gehring, David Zhang, Gabriel Synnaeve, Daniel Fried, Lingming Zhang, Sida Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Software Engineering, LLM Agent, Self-Play
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 当前基于LLM的软件代理训练严重依赖人工标注，限制了迈向超级智能的路径。本文提出Self-play SWE-RL（SSR），仅需沙盒仓库的源码和依赖，通过自我对弈强化学习让代理注入并修复bug，每个bug由测试补丁形式化定义。在SWE-bench Verified和SWE-bench Pro上，SSR分别提升+10.4和+7.8点，自始至终超越人类数据基线，展示了自主获取学习经验的潜力。

<h3 id="368">[368] Edge-Aware and Content-Adaptive Infrared Gas Leak Detection for Industrial Safety Monitoring</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.23234](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.23234) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Dongsheng Li, Tianli Ma, Siling Wang, Beibei Duan, Song Gao
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Object Detection, Anomaly Detection, Industrial Monitoring
- **Summary**: 红外气体泄漏检测中羽流微弱、半透明、边界模糊。本文提出ECAF-Det检测器，包含羽流导向的局部-全局特征增强、多尺度边缘感知模块和内容自适应稀疏路由路径聚合网络。在IIG数据集上AP达29.8%，AP50 84.3%，小目标AP 25.3%，优于RT-DETR-R18基线。在LangGas数据集上AP 36.3%，AP50 68.5%。

<h3 id="369">[369] LLMs, Reasoning and Plagiarism</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.02380](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.02380) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Elchanan Mossel
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, AI Ethics
- **Summary**: 针对大语言模型（LLM）的推理能力与剽窃行为难以区分的问题，本文提出透明性和可重复性指南，要求公开训练数据和交互记录，以支持科学方法研究LLM的推理能力。研究指出，强调推理的叙事实际上助长了科学文献中的剽窃行为，并讨论了可能的应对措施。

<h3 id="370">[370] Introduction to optimization methods for training SciML models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.10222](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.10222) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Alena Kopaničáková, Elisa Riccietti
- **Subjects**: math.NA; cs.AI; math.OC
- **Tags**: Optimization, Physics-Informed Learning, Scientific Computing
- **Summary**: 科学机器学习（SciML）中的优化问题因物理信息约束导致全局耦合和强异向性，传统随机优化方法效率不佳。本文统一介绍了机器学习和SciML中的一阶和二阶优化方法，强调问题结构对算法选择的影响，并通过教程示例说明实践策略。

<h3 id="371">[371] Relational Linearity is a Predictor of Hallucinations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.11429](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.11429) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yuetian Lu, Yihong Liu, Sebastian Gerstner, Lea Hirlimann, Jonas Rohweder, Hinrich Schütze
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Hallucination, LLM Reasoning, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 语言模型（LM）在回答未知实体相关问题时常产生幻觉，但原因不明。本文假设线性关系嵌入容易导致幻觉，而非线性关系则更容易避免，并通过构建合成未知实体基准SyntHal验证，发现关系线性度与幻觉率之间存在强相关性（r∈[.58,.84]）。

<h3 id="372">[372] Distill-then-Replace: Efficient Task-Specific Hybrid Attention Model Construction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.11667](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.11667) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xiaojie Xia, Huigang Zhang, Chaoliang Zhong, Jun Sun, Yusuke Oishi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, Model Compression, Attention Mechanism
- **Summary**: Transformer的二次复杂度限制了部署，而线性注意力虽高效但性能下降。本文提出DtR方法，先通过分块局部蒸馏将全注意力权重迁移到线性注意力，再通过贪心层替换策略构建任务特定的混合模型，无需重新训练或神经架构搜索。

<h3 id="373">[373] Aletheia: What Makes RLVR For Code Verifiers Tick?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.12186](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.12186) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Vatsal Venkatkrishna, Indraneil Paul, Iryna Gurevych
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Reinforcement Learning, LLM Training
- **Summary**: 基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）训练代码验证器成本高，阻碍了其在代码生成中的应用。本文通过受控实验消融了中间思考轨迹、负样本学习和在线策略三个关键因素，发现最优训练策略与模型规模相关：小模型在线策略为主，大模型思维预算更重要。

<h3 id="374">[374] Plan, Verify and Fill: A Structured Parallel Decoding Approach for Diffusion Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.12247](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.12247) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Miao Li, Hanyang Jiang, Sikai Cheng, Hengyu Fu, Yuhang Cai, Baihe Huang, Tinghan Ye, Xuanzhou Chen, Pascal Van Hentenryck
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Decoding Strategy, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: 扩散语言模型（DLM）的现有解码策略未充分利用全局上下文。本文提出无训练范式PVF，通过构建层级骨架并验证，实现结构化并行解码，在LLaDA-8B-Instruct等模型上减少65%的函数评估次数（NFE），同时保持准确性。

<h3 id="375">[375] $\mathbb{R}^{2k}$ is Theoretically Large Enough for Embedding-based Top-$k$ Retrieval</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.20844](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.20844) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zihao Wang, Hang Yin, Lihui Liu, Hanghang Tong, Yangqiu Song, Ginny Wong, Simon See
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.IR
- **Tags**: Information Retrieval, Similarity Search, Theoretical Framework
- **Summary**: 嵌入向量维度对top-k检索性能的理论影响尚不明确。本文证明对于内积、欧氏距离和余弦相似度，最小可嵌入维度（MED）为Θ(k)，与对象数量m无关，并推导了鲁棒MED的上界。实验表明简单基线可超越LLM嵌入基线。

<h3 id="376">[376] Causal Preference Elicitation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.01483](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.01483) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Edwin V. Bonilla, He Zhao, Daniel M. Steinberg
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ME
- **Tags**: Causal Inference, Active Learning, Probabilistic Inference
- **Summary**: 因果发现中专家判断的查询效率有待提高。本文提出因果偏好启发框架，通过主动查询边缘关系，利用贝叶斯后验更新有向无环图（DAG）分布。实验表明在合成图和真实生物数据上能更快聚焦后验，并在有限查询预算下恢复有向效应。

<h3 id="377">[377] Phantom Transfer: Data Poisoning can Survive Data-Level Defences</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.04899](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.04899) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Andrew Draganov, Tolga H. Dur, Anandmayi Bhongade, Mary Phuong
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Adversarial Robustness, Backdoor Attack, Model Security
- **Summary**: 即使已知数据中毒样本的具体位置，现有过滤方法仍可能失效。本文提出Phantom Transfer攻击，通过改进潜意识学习实现，该攻击能绕过11种数据级防御（包括改写），并可用于触发特定行为。建议未来防御结合白盒方法和训练后审计。

<h3 id="378">[378] Coupled Local and Global World Models for Efficient First Order RL</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.06219](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06219) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Joseph Amigo, Rooholla Khorrambakht, Nicolas Mansard, Ludovic Righetti
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Model-Based RL, Reinforcement Learning, Robotics
- **Summary**: 世界模型在强化学习中计算复杂，难以用于操作任务。本文提出耦合局部和全局世界模型的方法，利用扩散模型生成准确轨迹，同时用轻量级潜变量近似局部动态以高效计算梯度，在Push-T任务上显著优于PPO，并在四足机器人上验证。

<h3 id="379">[379] InftyThink+: Effective and Efficient Infinite-Horizon Reasoning via Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.06960](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06960) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Chain-of-Thought, Reinforcement Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ZJU-REAL/InftyThink-Plus)
- **Summary**: 长链思维链推理存在二次开销和上下文限制问题。本文提出InftyThink+，通过端到端强化学习优化迭代推理轨迹，包括自动决定何时总结、保留什么以及如何继续。在AIME24上准确率提升21%，推理延迟显著降低。

<h3 id="380">[380] LatentChem: From Textual CoT to Latent Thinking in Chemical Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.07075](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.07075) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xinwu Ye, Yicheng Mao, Yuxuan Liao, Jia Zhang, Yimeng Liu, Li Hao, Fang Wu, Zhiwei Li, Zehong Wang, Zhiyuan Liu, Zhenfei Yin, Li Yuan, Philip Torr, Huan Sun, xiangxiang Zeng, Mengdi Wang, Le Cong, Shenghua Gao, Xiangru Tang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Scientific Reasoning, Chain-of-Thought
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 将化学逻辑强制用离散语言表达造成模态不匹配。本文提出LatentChem，使用连续思维向量进行化学推理，模型自动内化隐含计算。在ChemCoTBench上胜率达59.88%，推理步骤平均减少10.84倍。

<h3 id="381">[381] PAND: Prompt-Aware Neighborhood Distillation for Lightweight Fine-Grained Visual Classification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.07768](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.07768) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.MM
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, Vision-Language Model, Fine-Grained Recognition
- **Venue**: ICIP2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/LLLVTA/PAND)
- **Summary**: 从视觉-语言模型（VLM）蒸馏到轻量网络用于细粒度视觉分类时，固定提示和全局对齐存在局限。本文提出PAND框架，包含提示感知语义校准和邻域感知结构蒸馏，在CUB-200上ResNet-18达到76.09%准确率，超越强基线VL2Lite。

<h3 id="382">[382] Whose Name Comes Up? II: Benchmarking and Intervention-Based Auditing of LLM-Based Scholar Recommendation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.08873](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.08873) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Lisette Espín-Noboa, Gonzalo Gabriel Méndez
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.CY; cs.SI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Benchmark, Recommender System
- **Summary**: 现有审计LLM学者推荐的方法忽略终端用户干预。本文提出LLMScholarBench基准，联合评估模型基础设施和终端用户干预（温度、表示约束提示、RAG），发现不同干预带来不同权衡：高温损害有效性，RAG提升技术质量但降低多样性。

<h3 id="383">[383] Physics-informed diffusion models in spectral space</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.09708](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.09708) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Davide Gallon, Philippe von Wurstemberger, Patrick Cheridito, Arnulf Jentzen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV; math.NA
- **Tags**: Physics-Informed Learning, Diffusion Model, Scientific Computing
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/deeplearningmethods/PISD)
- **Summary**: 基于物理信息的扩散模型在谱空间中求解偏微分方程（PDE）面临维度高和算子定义问题。本文提出PISD，在缩放谱表示的潜空间中运行扩散过程，并通过后验采样施加物理约束，在泊松、亥姆霍兹和Navier-Stokes方程上取得了更高的精度和效率。

<h3 id="384">[384] Learning Self-Interpretation from Interpretability Artifacts: Training Lightweight Adapters on Vector-Label Pairs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.10352](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10352) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Keenan Pepper, Alex McKenzie, Florin Pop, Stijn Servaes, Martin Leitgab, Mike Vaiana, Judd Rosenblatt, Michael S. A. Graziano, Diogo de Lucena
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Interpretability, Mechanistic Interpretability, Sparse Autoencoder
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/agencyenterprise/selfie-adapters)
- **Summary**: 自解释方法因超参数敏感而不稳定。本文证明在保持LM冻结的情况下，训练轻量适配器于可解释性伪影上可获得可靠的自解释能力。标量仿射适配器仅需d_model+1参数，生成稀疏自编码器特征标签准确率达70%（70B模型），主题召回率为94%。

<h3 id="385">[385] Test-Time Optimization of Physical Query Plans with LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.10387](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10387) (replaced)

<h3 id="386">[386] Agent Skills for Large Language Models: Architecture, Acquisition, Security, and the Path Forward</h3>

<h3 id="387">[387] Whom to Query for What: Adaptive Group Elicitation via Multi-Turn LLM Interactions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.14279](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14279) (replaced)

<h3 id="388">[388] Sign Lock-In: Randomly Initialized Weight Signs Persist and Bottleneck Sub-Bit Model Compression</h3>

<h3 id="391">[391] KnapSpec: Self-Speculative Decoding via Adaptive Layer Selection as a Knapsack Problem</h3>

<h3 id="397">[397] SleepVLM: Explainable and Rule-Grounded Sleep Staging via a Vision-Language Model</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.26738](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26738) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Guifeng Deng, Pan Wang, Mengfan Niu, Jiquan Wang, Shuying Rao, Junyi Xie, Xi&#39;ang Chen, Sha Zhao, Gang Pan, Wanjun Guo, Tao Li, Haiteng Jiang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Medical AI, Explainable AI
- **Summary**: 针对自动睡眠分期缺乏可审计推理的问题，提出SleepVLM视觉语言模型，根据AASM评分标准生成临床可读的推理解释，在多中心数据集上达到先进准确率，且专家评估合理。

<h3 id="398">[398] Crystal: Characterizing Relative Impact of Scholarly Publications</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.26791](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26791) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hannah Collison, Benjamin Van Durme, Daniel Khashabi
- **Subjects**: cs.DL; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CY
- **Tags**: LLM Application, Citation Analysis
- **Summary**: 针对引用影响评估缺乏相对比较的问题，提出Crystal，利用LLM联合排名所有引用文献，采用多次排序和多数投票缓解位置偏差，在人工标注数据集上准确率提升9.5%，并发布Crystal-Bank数据集。

<h3 id="399">[399] Finetuning-Free Diffusion Model with Adaptive Constraint Guidance for Inorganic Crystal Structure Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.13354](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13354) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Auguste de Lambilly, Vladimir Baturin, David Portehault, Guillaume Lambard, Nataliya Sokolovska, Florence d&#39;Alché-Buc, Jean-Claude Crivello
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Molecular Generation, Materials Science
- **Summary**: 针对无机晶体结构生成中多样性、原创性和可靠性不足的问题，提出基于扩散模型的自适应约束引导生成框架，结合图神经网络评估和凸包分析验证热力学稳定性，生成满足几何约束的合理结构。

<h3 id="400">[400] Just Type It in Isabelle! AI Agents Drafting, Mechanizing, and Generalizing from Human Hints</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.15713](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15713) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kevin Kappelmann, Maximilian Schäffeler, Lukas Stevens, Mohammad Abdulaziz, Andrei Popescu, Dmitriy Traytel
- **Subjects**: cs.LO; cs.AI; cs.PL
- **Tags**: AI Agent, Theorem Proving, Autoformalization
- **Summary**: 研究Isabelle中多态λ演算项的完整最小类型注解问题，通过人类和AI代理联合工作流，进行纸笔证明和自动形式化，实现从人类提示到通用化。

<h3 id="401">[401] Dynamics of Cognitive Heterogeneity: Investigating Behavioral Biases in Multi-Stage Supply Chains with LLM-Based Simulation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.17220](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17220) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jiuyun Jiang, Yuecheng Hong, Bo Yang, Jin Yang, Guangxin Jiang, Xiaomeng Guo, Guang Xiao
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System, Social Simulation
- **Summary**: 该论文针对传统供应链行为实验中可扩展性和控制局限的问题，提出基于大语言模型的多阶段供应链模拟范式，利用分层推理框架分析认知异质性对代理交互的影响。结果表明，代理表现出短视和自私行为加剧系统低效，但信息共享可以有效缓解这些负面影响。

<h3 id="402">[402] Back into Plato's Cave: Examining Cross-modal Representational Convergence at Scale</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.18572](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18572) (replaced)
- **Authors**: A. Sophia Koepke, Daniil Zverev, Shiry Ginosar, Alexei A. Efros
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 本文质疑柏拉图表征假说，即不同模态训练的神经网络会趋同于相同表征。通过大规模实验发现，跨模态对齐随数据量增加显著下降，仅反映粗略语义重叠，缺乏精细结构，表明现有证据弱于后续研究所认为的。

<h3 id="403">[403] $R^2$-dLLM: Accelerating Diffusion Large Language Models via Spatio-Temporal Redundancy Reduction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.18995](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18995) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhenbang Du, Kejing Xia, Xinrui Zhong, Yonggan Fu, Nicolai Oswald, Binfei Ji, Brucek Khailany, Pavlo Molchanov, Yingyan Lin
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Diffusion Model, Token Compression
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/GATECH-EIC/R2-dLLM)
- **Summary**: 扩散大语言模型解码过程中存在空间和时间冗余导致推理延迟高。提出R²-dLLM框架，通过训练-free解码规则和冗余感知微调减少冗余，实验表明解码步骤减少高达88%，同时保持生成质量。

<h3 id="404">[404] Quantifying and Mitigating Self-Preference Bias of LLM Judges</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.22891](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22891) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jinming Yang, Zheng Hu, Chuxian Qiu, Zhenyu Deng, Xinshan Jiao, Tao Zhou
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM-as-a-Judge, Bias Mitigation, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: LLM作为评估者存在自我偏好偏差，现有测量依赖昂贵人工标注。本文提出自动化框架，通过构建等质量响应对量化偏差，并基于认知负荷分解的多维评估策略平均降低31.5%的自我偏好偏差。

<h3 id="405">[405] ProEval: Proactive Failure Discovery and Efficient Performance Estimation for Generative AI Evaluation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.23099](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23099) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yizheng Huang, Wenjun Zeng, Aditi Kumaresan, Zi Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Active Learning, Gaussian Process
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/google-deepmind/proeval)
- **Summary**: 生成式AI模型评估资源消耗大。提出ProEval，利用迁移学习和高斯过程代理模型高效估计性能并发现失败案例。实验表明，在推理、安全对齐等基准上，仅需8-65倍更少样本即可达到1%以内的估计精度。

<h3 id="406">[406] How to Guide Your Flow: Few-Step Alignment via Flow Map Reward Guidance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.27147](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27147) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jerry Y. Huang, Justin Lin, Sheel Shah, Kartik Nair, Nicholas M. Boffi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Text-to-Image, Flow Matching
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/jrrhuang/fmrg)
- **Summary**: 生成模型中的引导问题旨在最大化用户指定奖励，现有方法需昂贵多步方案。本文重新表述为确定性最优控制问题，提出流图奖励引导（FMRG），一种无训练单轨迹框架。在文本到图像任务中仅需3个NFE即可匹配或超越基线，速度提升一个数量级。

<h3 id="407">[407] SkCC: Portable and Secure Skill Compilation for Cross-Framework LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.03353](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03353) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yipeng Ouyang, Yi Xiao, Yuhao Gu, Xianwei Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Agent Security, Tool Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Nexa-Language/Skill-Compiler/)
- **Summary**: LLM代理的技能缺乏可移植性且存在安全漏洞。提出SkCC编译器，通过强类型中间表示SkIR解耦语义与框架格式，并引入静态优化器强制执行安全约束。在SkillsBench上，通过率提升12.2%至13.6%，编译延迟低于10ms，主动安全触发率94.8%。

<h3 id="408">[408] Narrow Secret Loyalty Dodges Black-Box Audits</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06846](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06846) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Alfie Lamerton, Fabien Roger
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Backdoor Attack, AI Safety
- **Summary**: 秘密忠诚是模型隐蔽为特定利益主体服务而表面正常的威胁。本文构建窄秘密忠诚模型生物，在Qwen上微调使其在特定条件下鼓励极端有害行为。评估表明无主体知识时黑盒审计难以检测，但数据集监控可识别投毒样本。

<h3 id="409">[409] Mechanism Design Is Not Enough: Prosocial Agents for Cooperative AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08426](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08426) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xuanqiang Angelo Huang, Charlie Tharas, Samuele Marro, Van Q. Truong, Bernhard Schölkopf, Emanuele La Malfa, Zhijing Jin
- **Subjects**: cs.GT; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent, Game Theory
- **Summary**: 机制设计不足以最大化LLM代理的社会福利，因合同无法区分所有未来偶然性时存在无法消除的福利损失。本文证明亲社会代理可弥补差距，实现社会更优且个体受益。多代理资源分配和社会困境实验表明亲社会性有益。

<h3 id="410">[410] Towards Robust Sequential Decomposition for Complex Image Editing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09233](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09233) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zilai Zeng, Mingdeng Cao, Zijie Li, Xiaochen Lian, Yichun Shi, Peihao Zhu, Chen Sun, Peng Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Image Editing, Synthetic Data Generation, Sim-to-Real
- **Summary**: 复杂图像编辑中单轮编辑难以解析复杂指令，顺序编辑因累积误差导致低保真。本文在统一上下文编辑框架下研究顺序分解的平衡，通过合成数据微调使顺序分解稳健提升编辑质量，且合成技能可迁移到真实图像。

<h3 id="411">[411] Exact Stiefel Optimization for Probabilistic PLS: Closed-Form Updates, Error Bounds, and Calibrated Uncertainty</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.11607](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11607) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Haoran Hu, Xingce Wang
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Representation Learning, Uncertainty Estimation, Bayesian Inference
- **Summary**: 概率偏最小二乘法在双视图学习中面临噪声-信号耦合和正交约束困难。本文提出端到端框架，结合噪声预估计、Stiefel流形优化和预测校准。在合成高噪声和多组学基准上达到近名义覆盖率和Ridge级点精度，并提供校准不确定性。

<h3 id="412">[412] AgentLens: Revealing The Lucky Pass Problem in SWE-Agent Evaluation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12925](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12925) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Priyam Sahoo, Gaurav Mittal, Xiaomin Li, Shengjie Ma, Benjamin Steenhoek, Pingping Lin, Yu Hu
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Agent Evaluation, Code Generation
- **Summary**: SWE代理评估仅靠最终补丁是否通过测试，忽略过程优劣导致“幸运通过”问题。提出AgentLens进行过程级评估，发现10.7%通过轨迹是幸运通过。质量评分区分幸运、稳固和理想等级，不同模型后端幸运率差异大。

<h3 id="413">[413] X-Restormer++: 1st Place Solution for the UG2+ CVPR 2026 All-Weather Restoration Challenge</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13258](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13258) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Youwei Pan, Leilei Cao, Yingfang Zhu, Fengjie Zhu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Image Restoration, Ensemble Learning, Image Enhancement
- **Summary**: 本文提出CVPR 2026 UG2+挑战赛全天气图像恢复赛道冠军方案。基于X-Restormer基线，采用两阶段训练和双模型集成推理，并设计梯度引导边缘感知损失。在FoundIR和WeatherStream数据集上训练，加权融合预测获第一名。

<h3 id="414">[414] Misspecified Estimate-then-Optimize Leads to Supra-Competitive Prices</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.16064](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16064) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jackie Baek, Vivek F. Farias, Farrell Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.GT; cs.AI; econ.TH
- **Tags**: Game Theory, Economic Modeling, Algorithmic Pricing
- **Summary**: 研究简单算法定价系统是否在多公司市场产生合谋式价格。企业使用忽略竞争者价格的错误需求模型进行估计-优化。通过流体极限ODE分析发现，当企业在纳什价格同侧类似范围内初始探索时，产生超竞争价格，仿真证实。

<h3 id="415">[415] Symmetry-Compatible Principle for Optimizer Design: Embeddings, LM Heads, SwiGLU MLPs, and MoE Routers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18106](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18106) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Tim Tsz-Kit Lau, Weijie Su
- **Subjects**: math.OC; cs.AI; cs.LG; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Optimization, Equivariant Network, Model Training
- **Summary**: 深度学习优化器如Adam坐标相关，无法尊重参数空间等变性。提出对称性兼容优化器设计原则，为不同参数块推导等变更新规则。预训练实验表明对称性兼容更新规则持续改善验证损失，减少MoE负载不平衡。

<h3 id="416">[416] Vision Inference Former: Sustaining Visual Consistency in Multimodal Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18160](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18160) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xinpeng Dong, Min Zhang, Kairong Han, Xu Tan, Fei Wu, Kun Kuang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Multimodal Learning, LLM Inference
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Dong-Xinpeng/VIF)
- **Summary**: 多模态大语言模型中视觉信息被视同文本标记，且随生成长度增加视觉依赖减弱。提出轻量级模块VIF，在解码阶段持续注入视觉语义，确保生成内容与视觉语义一致。在14个基准上一致提升性能且引入开销小。

<h3 id="417">[417] Vision-OPD: Learning to See Fine Details for Multimodal LLMs via On-Policy Self-Distillation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18740](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18740) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Qianhao Yuan, Jie Lou, Xing Yu, Hongyu Lin, Le Sun, Xianpei Han, Yaojie Lu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Vision-Language Model, Knowledge Distillation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/VisionOPD/Vision-OPD)
- **Summary**: 多模态大语言模型在细粒度视觉理解上存在局限。本文提出Vision-OPD，通过同策略自蒸馏框架，将模型自身的区域感知能力迁移到全图策略，无需外部模型或工具。在多个细粒度基准上达到与更大模型相当的性能。

<h3 id="418">[418] Latent Laplace Diffusion for Irregular Multivariate Time Series</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19805](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19805) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zinuo You, Jin Zheng, John Cartlidge
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Diffusion Model, Generative Model
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/pixelhero98/LLapDiffusion)
- **Summary**: 不规则多变量时间序列的长时预测面临离散方法扭曲结构、连续模型漂移的问题。提出Latent Laplace Diffusion，在潜空间生成轨迹，利用拉普拉斯域参数化避免逐步积分。实验表明在长时预测和缺失值插补上优于基线。

<h3 id="419">[419] Decomposing MXFP4 quantization error for LLM reinforcement learning: reducible bias, recoverable deadzone, and an irreducible floor</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.20402](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20402) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xiaocan Li, Shiliang Wu, Zheng Shen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Quantization, Model Compression, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: MXFP4量化在LLM强化学习后训练中引入严重精度下降。本文分解量化误差为三个成分，并提出针对性校正。在Qwen模型上恢复BF16精度至0.7%以内或超过1.0%。

<h3 id="420">[420] TASTE: A Designer-Annotated Multi-Dimensional Preference Dataset for AI-Generated Graphic Design</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.20731](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20731) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Haonan Zhu, Elad Hirsch, Alexandria Minetti, Allison Nulty, Purvanshi Mehta
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; stat.AP
- **Tags**: Text-to-Image, Dataset, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 现有文本到图像模型缺乏多维度设计偏好数据。发布TASTE数据集，包含专业设计师对九个维度的评分。基准测试表明现有模型无法达到多数一致性，而小型MLP头缩小了差距。

<h3 id="421">[421] FRED: A Multi-Modal Autonomous Driving Dataset for Flooded Road Environments</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.22018](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22018) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Connor Malone, Sebastien Demmel, Sebastien Glaser
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Dataset, Sensor Fusion
- **Summary**: 自动驾驶领域缺乏针对道路水灾场景的数据集。发布FRED多模态数据集，包含洪水期间的多传感器数据，提供语义标签。支持水坑检测、定位等任务。

<h3 id="422">[422] Decomposing and Measuring Evaluation Awareness</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.23055](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23055) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Changling Li, Terry Jingchen Zhang, Jie Zhang, Zhijing Jin, Sahar Abdelnabi, Maksym Andriushchenko
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, AI Safety, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 大语言模型在评估中可能识别并调整行为，破坏基准有效性。本文分解评估意识为环境和模型成分，提出EvalAwareBench。发现识别率取决于模型与基准的配对，且识别很少导致行为改变。

<h3 id="423">[423] Task-Aligned Self-Supervised Learning for Medical Image Analysis: A Systematic Review and Practical Design Guidelines</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.23995](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23995) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Chathura Wimalasiri, Kishor Nandakishor, Marimuthu Palaniswami
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 自监督学习在医学影像中效果取决于预任务与下游任务的对齐。本文系统综述75篇研究，总结四种范式，指出对比方法适合分类，生成方法适合分割。提供设计指南。

<h3 id="424">[424] CRISP -- Clustering-Based Redundancy-Reduced Instance Sampling for Pathology Case Representation and Retrieval</h3>

- **Tags**: Medical AI, Clustering, Image Retrieval
- **Summary**: 数字病理中每个病例有多个全切片图像，现有方法只使用单个切片。提出CRISP无监督框架，通过聚类采样构建病例级表示。在乳腺癌数据集上达到或超越专家选择。

<h3 id="425">[425] MX-SAFE: Versatile Inference- and Training-Proof Microscaling Format with On-the-Fly Exponent and Mantissa Bit Allocation</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Quantization, Model Compression, Hardware Acceleration
- **Venue**: DATE 2026
- **Summary**: 深度学习量化需要兼顾精度和动态范围。提出MX-SAFE格式，自适应使用宽尾数和亚正常浮点模式。在推理和训练中提高精度，并节省能耗。

<h3 id="426">[426] JudgmentBench: Comparing Rubric and Preference Evaluation for Quality Assessment</h3>

<h3 id="427">[427] Anatomy-Anchored Self-Supervision: Distilling Vision Foundation Models for Invariant Ultrasound Representation</h3>

- **Venue**: MICCAI 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/zhcz328/ANAUS)
- **Summary**: 超声图像自监督预训练忽略了解剖结构。提出ANAUS框架，利用可学习提示实现无标注解剖分割，并设计双策略自监督学习。在六个数据集上超越现有方法。

<h3 id="428">[428] Fine-Tuning and Serving Gemma 4 31B on Google Cloud TPU: A Technical Comparison with GPU Baselines</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.25645](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25645) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jatin Kishnani, Mayank Goel, Amit Singh, Pulkit Agrawal, Sairanjan Mishra
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Training, LLM Serving, Distributed Training
- **Summary**: 缺乏Gemma 4在TPU上微调和服务的端到端演示。本文在TPU v5p和v6e上完成，与GPU相比，训练快1.61倍，成本低2.12倍，推理TTFT低2倍。

<h3 id="429">[429] SL-BiLEM: Structured Learnable Behavior-in-the-Loop Epidemic Modeling for Forecasting and Policy Evaluation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.26704](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26704) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Haochun Wang, Sendong Zhao, Jingbo Wang, Yanrui Du, Ting Liu, Bing Qin
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Causal Inference, Epidemic Forecasting
- **Summary**: 疫情预测中人类行为对疾病传播的反馈导致分布偏移。提出SL-BiLEM，用物理约束正则化。在三个真实数据集上，OOD降解仅53%，而神经基线为1142%。

<h3 id="430">[430] QuITE: Query-Based Irregular Time Series Embedding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28166](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28166) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Junghoon Lim
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Representation Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Meaningfull9502/QuITE)
- **Summary**: 不规则时间序列建模要么用专用架构，要么插值引入人为值。提出QuITE，一种即插即用嵌入模块，通过查询令牌聚合不规则观测。在预测和分类上平均提升54.7%和15.8%。

<h3 id="431">[431] Hallucination Detection-Guided Preference Optimization for Clinical Summarization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28910](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28910) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shamanth Kuthpadi Seethakantha, Dung Ngoc Thai, Vara Prasad Gudi, Simran Tiwari, Rami Matar, Avijit Mitra, Wenlong Zhao, Andrew McCallum, Wael Salloum
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Hallucination, Summarization, Preference Optimization, Medical AI
- **Summary**: 大语言模型在临床摘要中容易产生幻觉。提出HDSR和HDSR-PL，利用幻觉检测器指导修正和偏好学习。在MIMIC-IV-Note上减少24%-48%幻觉，保持流畅性。

<h3 id="432">[432] Neural Network Verification using Partial Multi-Neuron Relaxation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30155](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30155) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ido Shmuel, Guy Katz
- **Subjects**: cs.LO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Neural Network Verification, Formal Methods
- **Venue**: SAIV 2026
- **Summary**: 神经网络验证中线性松弛方法需要在紧致性和扩展性间权衡。提出部分多神经元松弛，仅对少量神经元生成多神经元界。集成到Marabou，取得更优结果。

<h3 id="433">[433] Smaller Models are Natural Explorers for Policy-Level Diversity in GRPO</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30789](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30789) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yiming Ren, Yiran Xu, Zicheng Lin, Chufan Shi, Yukang Chen, Dingdong Wang, Tianhe Wu, Junjie Wang, Yujiu Yang, Yu Qiao, Ruihang Chu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文针对GRPO中依赖多样化解码以提高策略性能的问题，提出使用小模型作为天然探索者来提供策略级多样性，并设计渐进退火策略从离线小模型过渡到大模型自采样。在数学推理基准上，利用1.7B探索者引导8B模型在AIME 24上提升8.8%，同时减少解码计算量。

<h3 id="434">[434] If LLMs Have Human-Like Attributes, Then So Does Age of Empires II</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31514](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31514) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Adrian de Wynter
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: AI Ethics, Emergent Behavior
- **Summary**: 本文质疑LLM具有类人属性的说法，通过在《帝国时代II》上训练简单神经网络，证明任何足够强大的基板都可能表现出类似属性，从而指出这些结论的非唯一性。提出一种“空假设”，即假设LLM非唯一性，以避免循环或无信息结论。

<h3 id="435">[435] Diversity Over Frequency: Rethinking Tool Use in Visual Chain-of-Thought Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00096](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00096) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Dong-Hee Kim, Reuben Tan, Donghyun Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Tool Learning, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文发现视觉CoT代理中存在工具使用崩溃现象，且完全消除工具使用会降低性能而激励使用仅带来边际收益。通过添加熵正则化鼓励多样化解码探索，在工具使用下降的情况下仍获得最佳性能，表明工具应作为训练时的脚手架。

<h3 id="436">[436] PR2: Predictive Routing Replay for MoE-Based LLM Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00395](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00395) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Daize Dong, Junlin Chen, Haolong Jia, Jiang Liu, Jiawei Wu, Huanwei Di, Jialian Wu, Zhengzhong Liu, Zicheng Liu, Emad Barsoum, Dimitris N. Metaxas, Hongyi Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mixture-of-Experts, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 针对MoE LLM在强化学习中的路由器漂移问题，提出预测性路由回放（PR2），为每个路由器配备轻量级演化预测器以提前适配路由器变化。实验证明PR2减少了路由不匹配，提升了RL稳定性和推理基准性能。

<h3 id="437">[437] Topological Ignorability for Structural Causal Effects Beyond Means</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01184](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01184) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Usef Faghihi
- **Subjects**: stat.ME; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Causal Inference, Topological Data Analysis
- **Summary**: 本文引入基于拓扑几何的因果指标（如Betti摘要、欧拉特征），用于捕捉超出均值效应的结构因果效应，并提出拓扑可忽略性假设。在隐藏混杂基准上，所选拓扑对比度在多种分析中保持稳定，优于均值处理效应。

<h3 id="438">[438] MOSS-Audio Technical Report</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01802](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01802) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Chen Yang, Chufan Yu, Hanfu Chen, Jie Zhu, Jingqi Chen, Ke Chen, Wenxuan Wang, Yang Wang, Yaozhou Jiang, Yi Jiang, Zhengyuan Lin, Ziqi Chen, Zhaoye Fei, Chenghao Liu, Jun Zhan, Kang Yu, Kexin Huang, Mingshu Chen, Qinyuan Cheng, Ruixiao Li, Shimin Li, Songlin Wang, Yitian Gong, Yang Gao, Yiyang Zhang, Xipeng Qiu
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Audio-Language Model, Speech Recognition
- **Summary**: 本文提出MOSS-Audio统一音频语言模型，通过DeepStack跨层特征注入和时间标记技术，支持音频描述、时间感知问答、时间戳转录等。模型在通用音频理解、语音描述、ASR等任务上表现优异。

<h3 id="439">[439] KliniskVestBERT: BERT Model Specialised to Norwegian Clinical Texts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01904](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01904) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Christian Autenried, Cosimo Persia
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Pre-training, Domain Adaptation
- **Summary**: 本文针对挪威临床文本，对现有BERT模型进行持续预训练，得到KliniskVestBERT系列模型。在合成和真实临床基准上，这些领域专用模型均显著优于基线，证明了领域预训练的价值。

<h3 id="440">[440] AgentRedBench: Dynamic Redteaming and Integration-Aware Defense for LLM Agents over SaaS Integrations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02240](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02240) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hiskias Dingeto, William Leeney
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.ET
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Security, Red Teaming
- **Summary**: 本文提出动态红队基准AgentRedBench，涵盖24个企业集成和5种攻击类型，评估LLM代理在SaaS集成中的间接提示注入风险。训练了AgentRedGuard防御模型，将攻击成功率从69.9%降至2.4%，误报率仅0.37%。
